Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The extraction variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 17 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 812 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 17 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the extraction structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 812. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 215.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on extraction structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 655. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 382 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the extraction decomposition. By minimality, the element a^655 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 382-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
